| `REMED-GFX-170` | **WebGPU and SDL_GPU reduced the nine-value `TextureFilter` enum to one boolean — DONE (2026-07-30).** The ticket said "the SpriteBatch sampler, in both backends, from the same one-line expression". The expression is right and the scope is wrong on the SDL_GPU half, measured not assumed. **AUTHORITATIVE TABLE, read from `include/Microsoft/Xna/Framework/Graphics/TextureFilter.hpp`, not from memory:** `0 Linear(min L, mag L, mip L)`, `1 Point(P,P,P)`, `2 Anisotropic(L,L,L + anisotropy)`, `3 LinearMipPoint(L,L,P)`, `4 PointMipLinear(P,P,L)`, `5 MinLinearMagPointMipLinear(L,P,L)`, `6 MinLinearMagPointMipPoint(L,P,P)`, `7 MinPointMagLinearMipLinear(P,L,L)`, `8 MinPointMagLinearMipPoint(P,L,P)`. **The magnification partition `{0,2,3,7,8}` and the minification partition `{0,2,3,5,6}` are DIFFERENT SETS** — that is precisely what one boolean cannot express, and no fixture had ever measured the second one. **THE EXACT FAILING EXPRESSION**, identical text in both backends: `filter = textureFilter == 0 ? LINEAR : NEAREST`, applied to min, mag AND mip alike (`WebGPUGraphicsBackend.cpp:4891/4894`, `SdlGpuGraphicsBackend.cpp:2742/2746`). **THE SECOND HALF, which the ticket did not name and which would have defeated a descriptor-only correction:** both cached the result in an 18-entry array indexed by `filterIndex = filter == 0 ? 0 : 1`, so all eight non-`Linear` ordinals shared ONE native sampler per address-mode pair — whichever was seen first decided the rest. Measured: 16 collapsed pairs, leg F1. **THE TWO BACKENDS ARE NOT THE SAME SHAPE.** WebGPU already carried a complete, correct table (`FillWGPUSamplerDescriptor`, splitting mag/min/mip and gating anisotropy) reached by every 3D family through `GetOrCreateSlotSampler`; exactly ONE call site bypassed it, the SpriteBatch bind group. **SDL_GPU has ONE sampler helper serving TEN call sites across nine draw families** — SpriteBatch, `Textured3D`, `LitTextured3D`, `AlphaTest3D`, `DualTexture3D` slots 0 and 1, `EnvironmentMap3D` base and cube, `Skinned3D`, `Pbr3D` — so there was no correct table anywhere to route to, and it **never set `enable_anisotropy` at all**, making `TextureFilter::Anisotropic` a plain nearest fetch and `ApplySamplerState`'s `maxAnisotropy` a stored value nothing read. **PRE-FIX SCORES on the new fixture, 70 checks: SOFTWARE 70/70 (the GFX-150-corrected reference), WEBGPU 56/70, SDL_GPU 41/70.** WebGPU failed only SpriteBatch legs and passed every 3D family; SDL_GPU failed those *and* all seven textured stock families — 41 vs 56 is the measurement that separates the two root causes. **FIX** = one authoritative translation per backend. WebGPU: delete `GetOrCreateSampler`, `samplerCache_` and `SamplerCacheIndex` outright and route SpriteBatch through the table the 3D families already use. SDL_GPU: add `FillSdlGpuSamplerCreateInfo`, an explicit per-ordinal table with **no default branch that silently means Point or Linear**, mirroring the tables Vulkan and WebGPU carry; replace the 18-entry array with a map keyed on the COMPLETE description `(filter, addressU, addressV, maxAnisotropy)`; capture `maxAnisotropy` into each 3D command next to the filter so a queued draw cannot observe a later `ApplySamplerState`, DualTexture keeping one per slot. `MaxAnisotropy` on the sprite path is XNA's `SamplerState` default (4) because `ISpriteBatchBackend::SetSamplerFilter` carries the filter ordinal alone — a constant, so trivially immutable per queued sprite. **A PRE-EXISTING LEAK CLOSED, not introduced:** WebGPU's destructor released the 18-entry sprite array but **never** `slotSamplerCache_`; consolidating without noticing would have made that the only leak. Both are one cache now, released once by the new `ReleaseSamplerCache`. **NO shader, NO bind-group layout, NO texture copy, NO extra pass/draw/submit/wait/frame, NO public API change, NO caching disabled, NO other backend and NO generated artifact touched** — four files. **NEW `examples/texture_filter_ordinal_contract_test.cpp`**, 70 checks, registered on all ten runnable backends. Point legs compare byte-exactly against the texel the contract selects; linear legs are proven by colours appearing in NO texel of an all-distinct pattern. Covers both partitions on SpriteBatch and on the device `SamplerStates[0]` 3D path, every textured stock family (plain/coloured/lit BasicEffect, AlphaTest, DualTexture slot 0, EnvironmentMap base, Skinned), sampler-cache distinctness across all nine ordinals in one frame, queued sampler transitions, Clamp/Wrap/Mirror preservation with byte-exact address-transformed texels and U/V independence, non-integer and odd-size magnification, interior source rectangles, and the 1:1 scale at which all nine ordinals must agree — the leg that names why a 1:1 fixture is structurally blind. **RESULTS: WEBGPU 56/70 -> 70/70, SDL_GPU 41/70 -> 70/70.** GFX-150's own fixture went **143/146 -> 146/146 on BOTH**. **NATIVE TRACES**, new and env-gated: `CNA_WEBGPU_SAMPLER_TRACE` and `CNA_SDLGPU_SAMPLER_TRACE` print draw family, ordinal name, mag/min/mip, anisotropy, address modes, cache key, native sampler handle and HIT/CREATE on one line per binding. **CARDINALITY, measured through them:** WebGPU 86 sampler applications -> **13** native samplers + 73 cache hits; SDL_GPU 90 applications across all nine families -> **14** + 76. Both are exactly the number of distinct sampler states the fixture uses — the minimum — so nothing is created per draw and the cache is still a cache. SDL_GPU's extra entry is `DualTexture3D/slot1`, a state WebGPU structurally cannot express (GFX-172). **MIP BOUNDARY, declared:** every texture in the fixture has ONE level, so each ordinal's mipmap half is asserted through the native descriptor in the trace, not through pixels; no mip-generation capability was added. **ANISOTROPY BOUNDARY, declared:** anisotropic output is unspecified per GPU, so only the LINEAR min/mag classification is required — which is the half that was broken. **VALIDATION:** WebGPU zero uncaptured errors and no device-lost across the matrix; SDL_GPU **zero VUIDs** with `VK_LAYER_KHRONOS_validation` proven loaded by the loader trace. **SANITIZERS:** ASan+UBSan clean on both, runtimes proven linked (`libasan.so.8`, `libubsan.so.1`), both 70/70 under them, **zero CNA-originating reports**; the residual LeakSanitizer output is Mesa/LLVM with no CNA frame and is A/B-proven pre-existing — byte-identical 624 B/6 allocations in the untouched `cna_test_sdlgpu_depth_bias`, and the untouched `cna_test_webgpu_clear_readback` leaks *more* (864 B) than the new fixture (368 B). **FALSE POSITIVES: 45 PRE-EXISTING fixtures registered on these two backends mention a sampler at all, and only 3 of them name a `TextureFilter` ordinal.** `stock_effect_sampler_contract_test` names `Linear` and `Point`; `webgpu_clear_readback_test` names `Point`; GFX-150's `point_sampling_contract_test` names 7 of the 9 — and for the four compound ordinals its leg Y asserts **only the magnification half**, by its own declared boundary. **`LinearMipPoint(3)` and `PointMipLinear(4)` were named by ZERO fixtures on either backend**, and the minification partition was measured nowhere. The gap was coverage, not correctness: no existing fixture asserts anything false, so none was changed or weakened, and the new one fills it. **REGRESSION GATES:** GFX-169's fixture unchanged at WEBGPU 64/65 (only the declared GFX-172 leg M3) and SDL_GPU 65/65; `sdlgpu_samplerstate_test` 7/7. **CROSS-BACKEND CONTROLS, only WebGPU and SDL_GPU production changed:** SOFTWARE **70/70** (the reference), VULKAN **70/70**, BGFX **70/70**, HEADLESS **1/1** asserting the GFX-127 rejection, EASYGL **50/70** (new finding GFX-174, not fixed and not worked around), D3D11 **70/70** and D3D9 **68/68** under Wine on `:99` (D3D9's two missing checks are its pre-existing D9-82d DualTexture layout decline, and GFX-171's half-pixel offset trips no leg here), D3D12 cross-built clean with the runtime unavailable for the pre-existing DX-100 reason. **FULL SHARDS:** `ctest -L WebGPU` **64/66** and `ctest -L SdlGpu` **67/68**, and every one of those three failures is either the declared GFX-172 leg or A/B-proven pre-existing by rebuilding the same test against the commit before the fix. **BOUNDARIES KEPT SEPARATE AND UNMODIFIED: GFX-171** (D3D9 half-pixel) and **GFX-172** (WebGPU DualTexture3D one sampler for two views) — and GFX-172's scope is **wider than its ticket says**: `EnvironmentMap3D`'s bind group has the identical `texEntries[0].sampler` + two views shape, so `SamplerStates[1]` is inexpressible for the reflection cube too. **TWO NEW FINDINGS: GFX-173 and GFX-174**, plus a scope correction to GFX-172. Commits: test `5a0aef7e`, fix (WebGPU) `98d97b32`, fix (SDL_GPU) `b7b6725e`, docs (this). | LOW | GRAPHICS | DONE |
| `REMED-GFX-171` | **D3D9 samples the 3D path half a destination pixel off — OPEN.** Found by REMED-GFX-150's cross-backend control: D3D9 scores **145/146**, failing only the non-integer 3D magnification leg (a 3x3 texture onto 10x10), and the failure is exactly a half-pixel offset rather than a filtering error. Every one of the 19 mismatching pixels sits at `s = 1.05` — just past the texel 0/1 boundary — and D3D9 returns the LOWER texel, i.e. it sampled as though the coordinate were about 0.5 destination pixels (0.15 texel at this scale) lower. That accounts for the count exactly: column `x=3` plus row `y=3` minus their shared pixel is `10 + 10 - 1 = 19`. The integer-scale leg (8x4 onto 16x8) passes only because a half-pixel shift lands it precisely ON a texel boundary, where the fixture accepts either neighbour — so the offset is present there too and merely unobservable. This is the classic Direct3D 9 pixel-centre convention (D3D9 places pixel centres at integer coordinates, unlike D3D10+/GL), uncompensated on the stock 3D path; D3D9's SpriteBatch legs all pass, so the compensation exists there and not here. Not fixed here: REMED-GFX-150 is scoped to Software production. | LOW | GRAPHICS | OPEN |

CNA's `DualTextureEffect::FillGpuDrawParams` confirms it (`Texture -> params.texture0`, `Texture2 -> params.texture1`). **THE EXACT PRE-FIX SHAPE, classified against the ticket's own A–E options: both families were A *and* D simultaneously** — `dual_texture3d.wgsl` and `env_map3d.wgsl` each declared one `@group(1) @binding(0) var texSampler: sampler` and passed it to BOTH `textureSample` calls (A); each group-1 layout had three entries, one sampler plus two views; and each `*DrawCommand` carried ONE `textureFilter/addressU/addressV/maxAnisotropy` group filled from `slotSamplers_[0]`, reused for both resources (D). **B and C were both FALSE and it mattered:** there is no second sampler binding anywhere to be unbound, and **there is no bind-group cache at all** — bind groups are built per draw and released with the frame, which the new trace records as `bgcache=none` rather than implying a key existed. `slotSamplers_[1]` was already populated by `ApplySamplerState`; only the `Queue*Draw` functions never read it. **THE FIX is one independently bound sampler per public slot, appended at `@binding(3)` so the two views keep the binding numbers every other WebGPU 3D family uses:** `tex1Sampler` for `DualTextureEffect.Texture2` in BOTH stride-20 and stride-24 shader modules, `envMapSampler` for the reflection cube, both group-1 layouts 3 entries -> 4, and both `Issue*Draw` resolving slot 1 through the EXISTING REMED-GFX-170 slot cache — no second translation table. **Nothing had to be invalidated:** the pipeline caches are already released and cleared by `Destroy{DualTexture,EnvMap}Resources` whenever the layouts are recreated, so a layout change cannot leave a stale pipeline. **MEASURED:** new `dualtexture_slot_sampler_contract_test` 58/106 -> **106/106**; `envmap_cube_sampler_contract_test` 74/104 -> **104/104**; `stock_effect_sampler_contract_test` 64/65 -> **65/65** (leg M3). Trace: 97 DualTexture3D and 86 EnvironmentMap3D rows, all `entries=4` and `slotMismatch=no`. **Cardinality:** sampler resolutions per draw 1 -> 2 (the minimum, one per sampled resource); native samplers 3 -> 12 only because slot 1's nine ordinals were previously never resolved at all, against 166 cache hits; draw ordering BYTE-IDENTICAL (262 enqueues, 89 passes, 86+6 issues) so no extra draw, pass, submit, frame, wait, copy or readback. Six cross-backend controls all score 106/106 (SDL_GPU, Vulkan, Software, Bgfx, EasyGL) or 6/6 (Headless), which is what shows the fixture pins a public contract rather than a WebGPU implementation detail. | LOW | GRAPHICS | DONE |
| `REMED-GFX-173` | **SDL_GPU hardcoded the `EnvironmentMapEffect` reflection-cube sampler, so `SamplerStates[1]` was ignored there — DONE (2026-07-31).** The ticket is RIGHT and names only HALF of it. **MECHANISM 2, the one it names:** `SdlGpuGraphicsBackend::IssueEnvMapDraw` bound `samplerBindings[1].sampler = GetOrCreateSampler(0, 1, 1, kSpriteBatchMaxAnisotropy, "EnvironmentMap3D/cube")` — a literal LinearClamp — under a comment calling it "this project's other backends' fixed reflection-map sampling convention". **MECHANISM 1, which it does not:** `QueueEnvMapDraw` captured only `samplerSlots_[0]`, so `EnvMapDrawCommand` had **no field in which slot 1 could reach replay at all**. Neither half alone is a fix: removing the constant without the capture makes replay read LIVE state and breaks the deferred-snapshot contract; adding the capture without touching the binding changes nothing observable. **AUTHORITATIVE SLOT MAPPING, read from source and not from the ticket:** `env_map3d.frag.glsl` declares `set=2 binding=0 sampler2D uTexture` and `set=2 binding=1 samplerCube uEnvMap`, and Vulkan's `GetOrCreateEnvMapDescSet` — corrected under REMED-GFX-169 — writes `slotSamplers_[0]` into binding 0 and `slotSamplers_[1]` into binding 1. So slot 0 filters `EnvironmentMapEffect.Texture` and slot 1 filters `EnvironmentMapEffect.EnvironmentMap`. **FIX** = four fields (`envMapFilter/envMapAddressU/envMapAddressV/envMapMaxAnisotropy`) captured BY VALUE at the public draw call beside the slot-0 fields already there, and the binding read from them — the identical shape `DualTextureDrawCommand` and `IssueDualTextureDraw` have carried three functions above since SDLGPU-21, which is the positive control the fix follows rather than a new design. The public->native translation is REMED-GFX-170's `FillSdlGpuSamplerCreateInfo` plus its complete-description cache key; **no second table**. **NO shader, NO bind layout, NO public API, NO texture copy, NO extra draw/pass/submit/wait/frame/readback, NO other backend** — two files. **NEW `examples/envmap_cube_sampler_contract_test.cpp`, 104 checks over thirteen legs, SDL_GPU 62/104 -> 104/104.** The oracle makes the two slots INDEPENDENTLY OBSERVABLE rather than inferring one from the other: with `EnvironmentMapAmount = 1`, `FresnelFactor = 0`, zero specular, white diffuse, alpha 1 and an all-opaque base texture the shader's `mix(baseColor, envSample.rgb * combinedAlpha, 1.0)` is EXACTLY `envSample.rgb`, so the cube alone decides every pixel; with amount 0 and ambient-only white lighting it is exactly `texColor.rgb`. Independence is therefore asserted as **byte-identical vs byte-different images**, not as a tolerance. Exactness comes from a generated palette whose entries are all >= 20 apart in L-inf and whose every pairwise midpoint is >= 9 from EVERY entry — **both properties are CHECKED (PAL2/PAL3), not assumed** — so "this pixel is a stored texel" and "this pixel is an interpolation" are decidable byte-exactly, and a constant sampler shows up as a Point image full of colours stored nowhere. Geometry: an ORTHOGRAPHIC projection with the eye at D = 4/3 makes `reflect(-E, N)` proportional to `(x, y, D)`, linear in the pixel and always +Z-major, so one cube face magnifies into a 4x4 grid of blocks; tilting the vertex NORMAL aims the same sweep at another face without touching UVs or shaders. **PRE-FIX NATIVE PROOF:** `CNA_SDLGPU_SAMPLER_TRACE` shows **ONE** distinct cube-slot description across **all 86** env-map bindings (`filter=0(Linear) mag=Linear min=Linear mip=Linear aniso=off addrU=ClampToEdge addrV=ClampToEdge key=0x04010100`) against **three** for the base slot. **POST-FIX: twelve**, exactly the distinct states the fixture uses. **ORDINALS 0..8 on the cube slot, all nine classified:** the four Point-magnifying ones (1,4,5,6) are byte-exact stored texels and agree byte-for-byte; the Linear ones (0,3,7,8) interpolate and agree byte-for-byte; `Anisotropic(2)` is asserted only for its magnification class, the same per-GPU boundary REMED-GFX-170 declared. **MIPMAPPED CUBE:** `TextureCube` allocation and level upload were VERIFIED not assumed (`num_levels = CalculateMipLevels`, per-level `mip_level` with `cycle=false`) and are correct — GFX-176's boundary holds. A real 6-level chain with one FLAT marker per (face, level) isolates the MIPMAP component: mip-Point reads stored levels only and reaches a level above 0, each mip-Linear ordinal differs from it, and a one-level cube minified identically still reads stored level-0 texels. The four mip-Linear ordinals are NOT required to agree — flat levels make min/mag invisible INSIDE a face but a cube is filtered seamlessly ACROSS face edges, which is a property of cube sampling and is declared. **ADDRESS-MODE BOUNDARY, classified honestly:** Clamp/Wrap/Mirror on a direction-addressed cube render IDENTICALLY here (0 differing pixels each), so the fixture asserts only that no mode produces an off-palette fetch, including a reflection aimed at a face boundary; **no cube address-mode convention is invented**. **CACHE/BINDING IDENTITY, measured through a new env-gated `CNA_SDLGPU_ENVMAP_TRACE`** that prints the whole two-slot binding on one line at BOTH ends of the deferred pipeline: the same cube under 12 distinct slot-1 descriptions receives 12 distinct native samplers, **no native sampler serves two descriptions**, and of the **43** draws whose slot-0 and slot-1 descriptions differ **none** shares one native sampler. **CARDINALITY:** 178 sampler applications before AND after; 86 queued env-map draws and 86 replayed, so no draw/pass/submit/frame is added; whole sampler cache 3 -> 12 native samplers (10 creations + 76 hits on the cube slot), the minimum; the base slot's 3 keys unchanged. **DRAW PATHS:** all eight — DrawPrimitives, indexed 16- and 32-bit, nonzero startIndex+baseVertex, DrawUser non-indexed and indexed 16/32-bit, and a twice-written dynamic buffer — each byte-identical to the reference under Point and interpolating under Linear. **VALIDATION:** `VK_LAYER_KHRONOS_validation` PROVEN inserted beneath SDL_GPU (`Insert instance layer "VK_LAYER_KHRONOS_validation"`), **zero VUIDs**, SDL_GPU debug mode on. **SANITIZERS:** ASan+UBSan 104/104 and UBSan-only 104/104, runtimes proven linked (`libasan.so.8`, `libubsan.so.1`), **zero ASan errors and zero UBSan runtime errors**; the residual 624 B / 6-allocation LeakSanitizer output has **zero CNA frames** and is byte-identical in the untouched `cna_test_sdlgpu_depth_bias`. **16 PRE-EXISTING SDL_GPU-REGISTERED FIXTURES DRIVE `EnvironmentMapEffect` AND ALL 16 PASSED ON THE UNFIXED BACKEND** — 8 zero the cube's contribution outright (`EnvironmentMapAmount = 0`, `stock_effect_sampler_contract_test`'s own comment says so), 7 use a cube that cannot express a filter difference (1x1 faces in `sdlgpu_envmap_test` and `rendertarget_effect_source_test`, flat-cleared RenderTargetCube faces elsewhere, or the SAME sampler on both slots), and `sdlgpu_samplerstate_test` names the family in prose while its slot-1 checks drive `DualTextureEffect`. **None asserts anything false, so none was changed or weakened**; the gap was coverage. **CONTROLS: VULKAN 104/104 and EASYGL 104/104** — two independent correct backends, which is what shows the fixture pins a public contract rather than one implementation — **HEADLESS 8/8** asserting GFX-127's rejection. Three divergences measured and classified, none fixed: **BGFX 62/104 (new REMED-GFX-181)**, **SOFTWARE 66/100 (new REMED-GFX-182)**, **WEBGPU 74/104 = the DECLARED REMED-GFX-172 boundary**, now measured rather than inferred — leg C1 FAILS there with differing=1024, so changing slot 0 changes the cube, the signature of one sampler entry serving two views. D3D9/D3D11/D3D12 cross-built clean; their Wine runtime controls are UNAVAILABLE on the permitted displays (`SDL_InitSubSystem(SDL_INIT_VIDEO) failed: No displays available` under Wine on a bare Xvfb, and `:0` is forbidden this session), reported as unavailable rather than clean. **REGRESSION GATES: SDL_GPU shard 71/72**, the single failure `SdlGpu_RenderState` being the PRE-EXISTING REMED-GFX-180, untouched and uninvestigated; GFX-169's, GFX-170's, GFX-175's and GFX-176's own fixtures, `SdlGpu_SamplerState`, `SdlGpu_EnvMap`, `SdlGpu_EnvMapEmissive` and `SdlGpu_DeferredSourceLifetime` all green. **TWO NEW FINDINGS: REMED-GFX-181 and REMED-GFX-182.** Commits: test `c34d0d00`, fix `5d28325d`, controls `35ba3323`, docs (this). | LOW | GRAPHICS | DONE |
| `REMED-GFX-174` | **EasyGL wrote the anisotropy component of a sampler only when it was ON, and one long-lived sampler object per slot made that a latch — DONE (2026-07-30).** The ticket recorded **two apparently independent components** and both readings are wrong: they are ONE defect, and the one the ticket describes in most detail is the one that was already fixed. **COMPONENT (2), the "unexplained" one, IS the root cause.** `EasyGLGraphicsBackend::ApplySamplerState` writes min, mag, wrapS and wrapT **unconditionally** but wrote `GL_TEXTURE_MAX_ANISOTROPY` **only inside the `filter == 2` branch**. `samplers_[slot]` is **ONE long-lived GL sampler object, mutated in place and reused** for every later application on that slot, so a write that can only ever RAISE the value leaves it raised forever: the first `Anisotropic` draw set it to `SamplerState`'s default `MaxAnisotropy` of **4** and every subsequent Point or Linear draw on that slot kept sampling anisotropically. Anisotropic taps average across the pixel footprint, so **no Point draw could return a stored texel again**. **PROVEN, not inferred:** leg D of GFX-170's fixture (8x4 minified to 3x2) returned **byte-identical output for all nine ordinals** — `(104,125,123)(135,125,122)(150,125,142)/(104,125,124)(135,125,128)(150,125,133)` — box averages of the source rows, with the sampler having literally no effect. The failing set names the mechanism exactly: **C0 and C1 pass and C4/C5/C6 fail** because leg C runs the ordinals in order and **ordinal 2 contaminates the slot part-way through**; every later point-magnifying ordinal fails and every linear one is unaffected. **WHY SPRITEBATCH LOOKED IMMUNE — ordering, not immunity.** `EasyGLSpriteBatchBackend` passes a **hardcoded `maxAnisotropy` of 1**, so the sprite path can never RAISE the value; but because the write happened only for ordinal 2 it could not LOWER it either, so **SpriteBatch never causes the contamination and still inherits it**. GFX-170's sprite legs (A, B) run BEFORE any 3D leg, which is the only reason they were green — the new fixture's legs F1..F3 run them after a 3D Anisotropic draw and **they fail pre-fix**. **COMPONENT (1) IS REFUTED AS STATED AND REAL SOMEWHERE ELSE.** Ordinals 2..8 do map onto GL `*_MIPMAP_*` minification filters, but that does **not** make an ordinary single-level texture incomplete: GL evaluates completeness over `[BASE_LEVEL, MAX_LEVEL]`, so a chain of ONE level is COMPLETE, and **Task 924 already clamps `GL_TEXTURE_MAX_LEVEL` to the real level count** for `EasyGLTextureBackend`. The new `CNA_EASYGL_SAMPLER_TRACE` reports `base=0 max=0 needsMipChain=1 complete=1` on **every** ordinary-texture draw. **Nothing clamped it for the other three sampleable kinds**, where GL's own default of 1000 stood — and a single-level `RenderTarget2D` sampled under any of the seven mip-chain ordinals rendered **SOLID BLACK, 128 of 128 pixels, on all seven**. So the ticket's mechanism is right, its example is the one case already fixed, and the real victims are render targets, cube maps and volume textures. **THE `MinFilter` THOSE CONSTRUCTORS ALREADY SET WAS DEAD COVER:** a sampler object bound to a texture unit **overrides** the texture object's own filters, and `ApplySamplerState` binds one for every slot on every draw, so the public `TextureFilter` — not the texture object — decides the effective min filter. Its comment ("since the RT has no mipmaps it would be texture-incomplete when sampled. Use LINEAR") was correct when written and stopped being true once sampler objects took over. **FIX** = two independent corrections. (a) write the anisotropy component on **every** application exactly like every other component, clamped to the extension's cap when the ordinal is Anisotropic and **1 otherwise**; (b) clamp `GL_TEXTURE_MAX_LEVEL` to `levelCount_ - 1` in `EasyGLRenderTargetBackend`, `EasyGLRenderTargetCubeBackend`, `EasyGLTextureCubeBackend` and `EasyGLTexture3DBackend`, mirroring Task 924. **Vulkan cannot have (a)** because it builds a fresh `VkSamplerCreateInfo` per sampler; the mutable shared object is what makes the unconditional write necessary. **NO forced mip allocation** — each constructor's storage loop already creates exactly `levelCount_` levels and the clamp only narrows an over-wide DECLARED range to the true one. **NO shader, NO UV change, NO CPU resampling, NO texture duplication, NO extra pass/draw/frame/submit/wait/readback, NO public API change, NO other backend and NO generated artifact touched** — one production file. **RESULTS: EASYGL 50/70 -> 70/70** on GFX-170's ordinal fixture, **unweakened**. **NEW `examples/sampler_component_isolation_contract_test.cpp`, 52 checks, 29/52 -> 52/52**, A/B-proven red-first by narrow file restoration. It is **ORDER-SENSITIVE by construction** — the legs that matter draw Anisotropic FIRST and then assert the next ordinal, with and without a flush between — which is the property every existing fixture lacked. It also asserts single-level completeness for **both** sampleable kinds, mip-level selection against a **real 4-level chain** whose levels are distinct flat colours (magnification stays on level 0 for all nine ordinals; mip-POINT minification selects exactly ONE stored level), sampler transitions, one-texture-many-samplers, many-textures-one-sampler, slot independence measured modulation-independently, and both SpriteBatch<->3D leak directions. **CARDINALITY, measured through the trace:** 1043 sampler applications and 65 3D draws in the new fixture, 566 and 32 in GFX-170's; **one** native sampler object per slot before and after, so nothing is created per draw. Cost is **one extra `glSamplerParameterf` per application** (only where the extension exists) and **one `glTexParameteri` per texture creation** — none per draw or per frame. **GL VALIDATION: 1803 traced events across both fixtures, ZERO GL errors** — every trace line drains `glGetError` at the event that would raise it. **SANITIZERS:** ASan+UBSan (`CNA_SANITIZE=address,undefined`, runtimes proven linked — `libasan.so.8`, `libubsan.so.1`) 52/52 and 70/70, **zero ASan errors and zero UBSan runtime errors**; the residual LeakSanitizer output is **byte-identical (100956 B / 449 allocations) in the UNTOUCHED GFX-170 fixture**, has **zero CNA frames** and is libGL/Mesa, matching GFX-170's own recorded baseline. **REGRESSION GATES:** `ctest -R EasyGL` **281/284**, the three failures exactly the ones Phase 0 classified as pre-existing (`EasyGL_DeviceValidation`, `EasyGL_GraphicsDevice_ReferenceStencil`, both A/B-proven in earlier records, and the external `easy-gl-resource-smoke-tests`). GFX-150 **146/146**, GFX-169 **65/65**, GFX-147 and GFX-168 green. **CROSS-BACKEND CONTROLS, only EasyGL production changed:** SOFTWARE **70/70** and **52/52**, VULKAN **70/70**, WEBGPU **70/70**, SDL_GPU **70/70**, BGFX **70/70**, HEADLESS **1/1** asserting the GFX-127 rejection. DirectX is structurally unreachable from this change and is declared rather than re-run — the changed file is not compiled into those configurations. **ONE NEW FINDING: REMED-GFX-175** (ordinals 0 and 1 carry no GL mipmap term, so a texture that owns a chain never mip-filters under `Linear` or `Point`) — measured and REPORTED by leg D4 as a note, deliberately **not** asserted to the wrong contract and **not** fixed here, and it reproduces on SOFTWARE too, so it is not an EasyGL question. **BOUNDARIES KEPT SEPARATE AND UNMODIFIED: GFX-171, GFX-172, GFX-173.** Commits: test `1b6a9b1b`, fix (anisotropy) `11e22980`, fix (completeness) `ca865bc5`, docs (this). | LOW | GRAPHICS | DONE |
| `REMED-GFX-175` | **A `TextureFilter` ordinal's MIPMAP component was dropped for `Linear` and `Point` on EasyGL — DONE (2026-07-30).** The ticket is RIGHT about EasyGL and WRONG about the second backend it names. **THE CONTRACT, settled from the reference implementation rather than inferred:** FNA's own decomposition tables (`src/Graphics/Effect/Effect.cs`, `XNAMin`/`XNAMag`/`XNAMip`) give **`Linear(0)` = min LINEAR, mag LINEAR, mip LINEAR** and **`Point(1)` = min POINT, mag POINT, mip POINT**, and FNA3D's GL driver (`XNAToGL_MinMipFilter`) maps exactly those two onto `GL_LINEAR_MIPMAP_LINEAR` and `GL_NEAREST_MIPMAP_NEAREST`. They are FULL filters, not "the ordinals without a mip component", and ordinal 0 is the DEFAULT filter every game gets. **EASYGL — the ticket's mechanism, confirmed exactly:** `ApplySamplerState` mapped 0 onto plain `GL_LINEAR` and 1 onto plain `GL_NEAREST`, neither of which carries a mipmap term, while ordinals 2..8 were already correct — measured, a 4x minification of a 4-level chain returned **level 0** for ordinals 0 and 1 and **level 2** for all seven others. **A SECOND EASYGL LAYER, found by the new fixture and required before the first fix was safe:** a `Texture2D` created with `mipMap=true` DECLARES a chain and Task 924 widens `GL_TEXTURE_MAX_LEVEL` to match, but the constructor only ever gave storage to **level 0** — so until the game happened to `SetData` every remaining level the texture was mipmap-INCOMPLETE and sampled as **opaque black over its whole surface, magnification included** (measured: 256/256 black pixels at a 4x MAGNIFICATION under every ordinal 2..8). That was already true before this task; giving 0 and 1 a mipmap term would have extended it to the default filter. **SOFTWARE — THE TICKET'S READING IS REFUTED.** The record says the same leg "reports the same result on SOFTWARE, so this is a cross-backend contract question". The symptom matches; the mechanism does not. **ALL NINE ordinals sampled level 0 here**, `LinearMipPoint`, `PointMipLinear` and the four `Min*Mag*` ordinals included — every one of which names a mip component explicitly. Software was not dropping a component: `SoftwareTextureBackend::UpdatePixelsLevel` was a documented **no-op**, so levels 1..N were discarded as the game supplied them, and the sampler had **no LOD stage at all**. Fixing ordinals 0 and 1 alone is not possible there; the missing machinery is shared by all nine. **FIX = three bounded corrections.** (a) EasyGL maps every ordinal through FNA3D's `MinMipFilter` table; (b) EasyGL gives every DECLARED level storage at creation and after context loss, exactly as its own render-target/cube/volume constructors and the reference driver's `CreateTexture2D` already do — this ALLOCATES what the caller asked for, it does not GENERATE it, and a `mipMap=false` texture is bit-for-bit unaffected; (c) Software stores the levels a caller supplies (never derived, never invented, counted only CONTIGUOUSLY from 0 so a declared-but-unwritten chain stays at one level) and its sampler gains a five-stage pipeline — footprint/LOD, clamp, mip selection, within-level filter, address mode. `TriangleMagnifies` became `TriangleTexelRate`, so REMED-GFX-150's magnification classification and the new LOD come from **one number** and cannot disagree. **NO shader change, NO UV or LOD-bias manipulation, NO CPU resampling, NO implicit mip generation, NO extra pass/draw/frame/submit/wait/readback, NO public API or enum change.** **RESULTS: new `examples/texture_filter_mip_contract_test.cpp`, 87 checks over 11 legs (Point LOD matrix, Linear LOD matrix, all nine ordinals, within-level+orientation, single-level completeness, declared-but-unwritten chain, SpriteBatch, transitions/recreation, NPOT+odd destinations, indexed/non-indexed/DrawUser). EASYGL 61/97 -> 87/87, SOFTWARE 65/97 -> 87/87.** At an integer lambda a trilinear filter degenerates to one level, so Linear is byte-exact there; between levels only the bounded "blends these two levels and nothing else" property is asserted. **No Point check is weakened to a tolerance.** **CROSS-BACKEND, measured: D3D9 87/87, D3D11 87/87, VULKAN 87/87, WEBGPU 87/87, BGFX 87/87 — five independent backends, including the real XNA-semantics one, already implemented this contract, which is what confirms the fixture pins XNA and not a local convention. D3D12 passed all 64 checks it reached before hitting DX-103's fixed descriptor-heap capacity (REMED-GFX-177). HEADLESS skips honestly. SDL_GPU 55/87 — an INDEPENDENT defect, ticketed as REMED-GFX-176, not absorbed.** **ONE FALSE-POSITIVE TEST FOUND AND CORRECTED:** `easygl_texture_mip_filter_effect_test.cpp` asserted `Point` samples RED at extreme minification and its header called that "a real, confirmed, documented deviation from FNA", naming the exact fix it was waiting for. It was pinning the defect as the contract; its Vulkan and Bgfx siblings have always expected GREEN. Now 2/2. **VALIDATION: 1550 traced GL events, `glerr=none` on every one, `complete=0` on none, ONE GL sampler object across 1380 applications (nothing created per draw), and all four observed effective min filters are mip-aware.** **SANITIZERS:** EasyGL ASan+UBSan (runtimes proven linked) 87/87, 70/70 and 52/52 with **zero ASan and zero UBSan errors**; the residual leak is byte-identical (100956 B / 449 allocations) in the UNTOUCHED GFX-170 fixture and has zero CNA frames. Software ASan and UBSan 87/87, 146/146, 52/52, all clean. **REGRESSION GATES: GFX-170 70/70 on EasyGL, Software, Vulkan, WebGPU, SDL_GPU and Bgfx; GFX-174 52/52 on EasyGL and Software; GFX-150 146/146.** Commits: test `b6838c22`, fix (EasyGL) `e6bdf211`, fix (Software) `daa6a20b`, test+docs (this). | LOW | GRAPHICS | DONE |
| `REMED-GFX-151` | **Vulkan: a `RenderTarget2D` sampled without an intervening `GetData` was empty because the readback flush never recorded the PRODUCER's render pass — DONE (2026-07-29).** The canonical XNA render-to-texture sequence — render into A, unbind, draw with A into B, read B — reproduced **0/32** with 14 texels entirely `(0,0,0,0)`; the fixture scored **15/43**. Vulkan is a whole-frame-deferred recorder, so REMED-GFX-074 added an early flush for a mid-frame `GetData` that records the read target's passes and consumes them. That flush filtered the frame's segment list down to the target being **READ** — a predicate on a draw's DESTINATION, which silently assumed a target's content depends only on draws issued INTO it. It also depends on every render target those draws **SAMPLE**, and a producer is a different target, so A's pass was erased and B's consumer pass ran against an image nothing had rendered into; A's pass was recorded later, at Present, after the read meant to observe it. **`GetData` was an accidental EXECUTION barrier, not a synchronization one:** `A.GetData()` runs the same flush for A first, so the missing native operation was the producer's render pass — not a wait, fence, transition or resolve. Everything else was already correct and was eliminated one by one against the running backend: the finalLayout is `SHADER_READ_ONLY_OPTIMAL`, the `COLOR_ATTACHMENT_OUTPUT`/`COLOR_ATTACHMENT_WRITE` → `FRAGMENT_SHADER|TRANSFER`/`SHADER_READ|TRANSFER_READ` exit dependency already existed at every render pass, the descriptor referenced the right `colorSampleView_` of the right image, the MSAA resolve is the pass's own resolve attachment and `MaybeGenerateMips` is emitted right after `vkCmdEndRenderPass`. **Two legs pinned it and both PASSED pre-fix:** the identical sequence with one `A.GetData()` inserted, and producer → **backbuffer** consumer (32/32 pre-fix), which goes through Present's ordered record and never through the filtered flush — so REMED-GFX-140/143's stream was already right. **Fix:** the flush computes the transitive closure of the bind cycles a readback depends on — seed with the read target's own group (REMED-GFX-074's set, plus REMED-GFX-142's whole-cube group), then pull in, for each cycle in the set, the earlier cycles of every render-target group it samples — and replays exactly that set, still in ascending segment order. The graph is `(bind cycle, sampled group)` recorded at enqueue time from the only four places a texture reaches this backend, keyed on `DepthStencilOwnerEXT()` so a dependency and the work satisfying it cannot drift apart. **A simpler positional rule (`replay every off-screen cycle up to the readback`) also turned the canonical fixture green and was implemented, measured and REJECTED:** it advances unrelated targets early, so a backbuffer draw still deferred to Present samples a target whose later cycle already ran — leg I2 reproduced 0/32 there and is exact under the closure. **No new synchronization of any kind:** no added barrier, fence wait, device/queue idle, submit per target switch, extra Present, extra frame, readback or CPU copy; the flush's submit-per-MRT-proxy loop is *replaced* by one command buffer and one submit, so cardinality goes DOWN. **15/43 → 43/43**, plus 43/43 on a `PreferMultiSampling` device with the applied sample count asserted to be 4, and 43/43 under **synchronization validation** with the layer proved live and **zero** messages of any kind. Full Vulkan shard **180/181**, the one failure (`Vulkan_DepthBias`) A/B-proven pre-existing. Cross-backend controls SOFTWARE 41/41, HEADLESS 29/29, EASYGL 41/41, BGFX 37/37, SDL_GPU 32/32, WEBGPU 38/38. ASan and UBSan clean. **8 of the 48 Vulkan render-target fixtures sample a target and all 8 observed the consumer through `GetBackBufferData`** — never through a target readback, the only path that reaches the filtered flush, which is exactly how this survived them; the archetype `vulkan_rt_roundtrip_test.cpp` was strengthened (2/4 → 4/4, A/B-proven). REMED-GFX-147's leg G0 declaration flipped to `true` everywhere. Spawned **GFX-155** and **GFX-156**. test `99cefc6f`, fix `8f3f63ca`, coverage `882aeb44` | HIGH | GRAPHICS | DONE |
| `REMED-GFX-152` | **SDL_GPU fabricated an `SDL_GPUTexture*` out of a render target's own fields whenever one was used as a 3D effect's texture — DONE (2026-07-29).** A TYPE defect, not synchronization, ordering, image layout, visibility or MSAA resolve. `SdlGpuTextureBackend` and `SdlGpuRenderTargetBackend` are unrelated **siblings** under `ITextureBackend`, and the former is `final`, so a render target can never be one — yet **thirteen** sites (the ticket said ten) did `static_cast<const SdlGpuTextureBackend*>(params.textureN)` and read `->Texture()` off the result. **Measured under UBSan, not inferred:** `downcast of address 0x55cb63b1f080 which does not point to an object of type 'SdlGpuTextureBackend' / note: object is of type 'SdlGpuRenderTargetBackend'` at `SdlGpuGraphicsBackend.cpp:3367`, then `member call` at `:4817`, then `member access` at `SdlGpuGraphicsBackend.hpp:119`, then **SIGSEGV (exit 139)**. The failing expression is `return texture_;` reading offset 16, which in the render target's layout is **`mipMap_` + three bytes of UNINITIALISED PADDING + `multiSampleCount_`** — `0x20612000` in the captured object, visible in UBSan's own hex dump, and handed straight to `SDL_BindGPUFragmentSamplers`. The queue-time cast is UB but harmless; the fatal read is at Present, because this backend defers every draw. **Why only the 3D legs died:** SpriteBatch's `Draw` and `QueueEnvMapDraw`'s cube slot already asked the object with `dynamic_cast`. One route asked, the others assumed. **Fix: one shared resolver**, `ResolveSampledTextureEXT`/`ResolveSampledCubeEXT`, the only place this backend decides what a texture resource is — `dynamic_cast`, empty for a null optional slot, and a **named** `std::invalid_argument` carrying the public API name for anything else, never a reinterpretation. Each returns an `SdlGpuSampledTextureEXT` (sampleable native handle + a `shared_ptr` keep-alive), which commands store instead of a wrapper pointer, closing the LIFETIME half as well. **MSAA is right by construction, not by a new step:** `Sampled()` returns the single-sample texture the multisample attachment resolves INTO, and the attachment is `COLOR_TARGET`-only, so it is not a legal sampler binding and can no longer be selected. Ordinary `Texture2D`/`TextureCube` gained the shared state render targets already had; **honest scope** — an immediate release was A/B-measured and produced neither a wrong pixel nor an ASan report (SDL_gpu defers destruction internally), so that half is hardening, not a defect observed failing. **Cost: nothing was added.** SDL call-site cardinality unchanged for texture creation (10), sampler binds (8), render passes (3), submits (7), command buffers (10), fence waits (4), downloads (4), samplers (1) and transfer buffers (11); `SDL_ReleaseGPUTexture` 15 → 12. No Present, GetData, CPU copy, readback, wait, extra frame/pass/submit. One `dynamic_cast` per binding, one extra `make_shared` per texture OBJECT, none per draw. **New process-isolated `rendertarget_effect_source_test.cpp`** — because the pre-fix failure is a SIGSEGV, not an assertion, it supervises each of eighteen legs in its own child (`--leg=<id>`) and classifies PASS/FAIL/**CRASH with the signal named**, so process death is attributable instead of costing the shard. Every consumer leg is a difference test against an ordinary `Texture2D` control through identical state in the same frame; the producer is never read. **15 of 18 legs SIGSEGV and 10 checks → 18/18 legs, 0 crashes, 56/56.** The three pre-fix survivors are exactly C1 (SpriteBatch), L1 (cube) and O1 (resolves nothing) — the routes that could not hit the defect. **Four false-positive skips removed, each A/B-measured:** `sampling_orientation` 5 PASS+SIGSEGV → 57/57 (was 51/51), `producer_consumer` 5 PASS+SIGSEGV → 36/36 (was 32/32), `backbuffer_consumer` 1 PASS+SIGSEGV → 4/4, and `first_use` **proven OVER-APPLIED** — its legs J and K bind an ordinary `Texture2D` and merely render INTO a target, so they PASS pre-fix; that skip hid nothing and cost three checks (17/17 → 20/20). **A fifth was found in this task's own fixture**: REMED-GFX-125's trap, `sizeof(VertexPositionTexture)` being 32 not 20 (polymorphic `IVertexType` vptr + padding) was being passed as the GPU stream stride, so the `VertexBuffer` legs declared a layout some backends read as `VertexPositionNormalTexture`; a packed 20-byte struct with a `static_assert` fixed it and D3D9 went 43/46 → 47/47 on that alone. **Native validation clean** in all eighteen children with SDL_GPU debug mode on, and again with `VK_LAYER_KHRONOS_validation` forced and proved loaded: zero VUID reports. **ASan and UBSan clean**, runtimes proved linked by symbol; UBSan is the direct A/B, 3 `runtime error` reports → 0. **Cross-backend controls, only SDL_GPU production changed:** SOFTWARE/HEADLESS/EASYGL/BGFX/VULKAN/WEBGPU **18/18** each, **D3D9 47/47 and D3D11 50/50 RUN under Wine on `:99`** (a Windows build has no `fork`, so the supervisor compiles out and legs run in-process), D3D12 **cross-build only** for the same pre-existing dxgi reason as its neighbouring contract fixtures. `ctest -R '^SdlGpu'` **85/86**, the one failure (`SdlGpu_RenderState`) A/B-proven pre-existing; GFX-127/134/141/143/145/156/157/160 gates all green; all fifteen backend libraries build incrementally. **Boundaries declared rather than skipped**, each naming its own pre-existing ticket: no custom-effect slot on SDL_GPU's stock 3D paths, one fixed native colour format, no `DrawInstancedPrimitives`, and vertex-layout limits on WEBGPU/D3D9/D3D11/D3D12 — and a leg that measures nothing must now SAY why or it fails. Spawned **GFX-166** (Vulkan loses a queued draw whose source was destroyed before the frame rendered) and **GFX-167** (WebGPU dies at teardown after a backbuffer consumer). test `48ec5347`, fix `c3d8cc0b`, false positives `b36aa7df`, cross-backend `d95baa9d` | HIGH | GRAPHICS | DONE |
| `REMED-GFX-176` | **SDL_GPU never gave a `Texture2D` more than one GPU mip level, so NO `TextureFilter` ordinal mip-filtered — DONE (2026-07-31).** The ticket is RIGHT, and there were **three** mechanisms, not the two it names. **(1) ALLOCATION:** `SdlGpuTextureBackend`'s constructor set `createInfo.num_levels = 1` as a literal and never read `ImageData::mipLevels` — the field `Texture2D`'s own constructor fills from `CalculateMipLevels(w, h)`. **(2) UPLOAD:** the class declared **no `UpdatePixelsLevel` override at all**, so it inherited `ITextureBackend`'s empty default and every `SetData(level > 0, ...)` was accepted by the shared layer, written to its CPU mip buffer, handed down and silently dropped — no error, no warning, no pixel. Both MEASURED, not inferred, by the new `CNA_SDLGPU_TEXTURE_TRACE`: against the unfixed backend, **77 native textures created with `nativeLevels=1` on every one** while `requestedLevels` ran up to 5, and **ZERO `level>0` upload commands ever emitted**. **(3) CYCLE — reachable only once the first two were fixed, and the new fixture is what found it.** The level-0 upload passed `cycle=true` to `SDL_UploadToGPUTexture` ("swap to a fresh internal resource rather than stall"), and a fresh resource does not carry the levels the upload is not writing. Harmless for one level, silently destructive for a chain. **A/B measured by retaining it: the storage fixture drops to 52/112 and GFX-175's to 41/87 — BELOW the 55/87 this task STARTED from.** A fix that allocated and uploaded but kept `cycle=true` would have regressed the backend while passing every storage check. `cycle` is now true only when `levelCount_ == 1`, keeping the `mipMap=false` path byte-identical, and false for a chain — the same conclusion SDLGPU-40 reached on the cube path and REMED-GFX-135 on the volume path. This is also why allocation and upload are ONE commit: allocating a chain without the upload leaves levels 1..N undefined, which is worse than the single level it replaces, so there is no useful intermediate state. **FIX:** one shared `UploadLevel` whose region, `mip_level`, transfer size, row pitch and `pixels_per_row` all come from the LEVEL's dimensions rather than the resource's, rejecting a level whose supplied extent is not that level's; non-positive extents and an over-long chain rejected BEFORE `SDL_CreateGPUTexture`. **NO mip generation** (`SDL_GenerateMipmapsForGPUTexture` is not called for a `Texture2D`), no CPU shadow, no extra frame/Present/wait/retry/readback, no public API or enum change. **RESULTS: new `examples/sdlgpu_texture2d_mip_storage_test.cpp`, 112 checks over 11 legs, 45/112 -> 112/112; REMED-GFX-175's own fixture 55/87 -> 87/87**, joining the eight backends already there. Levels carry ASYMMETRIC self-identifying patterns and each is read back BYTE-EXACT by rendering it at its own size under Point, so content, orientation, row pitch and extent are asserted at once with no tolerance. Matrix: 1x1, 2x2, 4x4, 8x8, 8x4, 16x2, 3x2, 5x3, 6x6, 7x5 — public `LevelCount` and native `num_levels` agree on every one, every axis clamps to >= 1, every final level is 1x1. **BOUNDARIES: `Texture2D` was the ONLY affected class** — `Texture3D`, `TextureCube`, `RenderTarget2D` and `RenderTargetCube` already passed a real `CalculateMipLevels` and already had level-aware uploads, so nothing is absorbed. `GetData` on a plain `Texture2D` is served by the shared layer's CPU shadow on EVERY backend and never reaches the backend, stated as leg K rather than implied; rendered sampling is the oracle. **GFX-173 untouched** (EnvironmentMap measured on its 2D slot only). **CARDINALITY:** 77 textures created / 77 destroyed (one native texture per public texture, none per level), 291 upload commands for 291 public level uploads, 19 `cycle=yes` (exactly the one-level textures) and 272 `cycle=no`, no extra submit/fence/pass/frame. **VALIDATION:** Vulkan validation PROVEN loaded beneath SDL_GPU (`Insert instance layer "VK_LAYER_KHRONOS_validation"`), **zero VUIDs** over 112+87+70+7 checks; SDL_GPU debug mode on, no error. **SANITIZERS:** ASan+UBSan and UBSan-only both 112/112 and 87/87 with zero errors; the 624 B / 6-allocation residual leak has zero CNA frames and is byte-identical in three untouched fixtures that build no chain at all. **ONE FALSE POSITIVE FOUND:** `texture2d_getdata_transfer_range_test.cpp` R12/R13 pass on the UNFIXED backend (A/B measured, 5 checks) because a no-op `UpdatePixelsLevel` cannot throw and the level-1 read comes from the CPU shadow — a blind spot, not a wrong assertion; left unchanged, and leg B is what now covers it. **CONTROLS: EasyGL, Software, Vulkan, WebGPU, Bgfx all 87/87**, Headless skips honestly; D3D9/D3D11/D3D12 cross-built clean and their runtime controls omitted since no shared code changed. SDL_GPU shard 70/71 — the one failure, `SdlGpu_RenderState`, is PRE-EXISTING (aborts identically on `249e9f9f` with the unmodified backend) and is filed as REMED-GFX-180. Commits: test `df1d5111`, fix `5ed3b126`, docs (this). | LOW | GRAPHICS | DONE |
| `REMED-GFX-177` | **D3D12 consumed a descriptor slot for the lifetime of the PROCESS instead of the resource — DONE (2026-07-30).** The ticket recorded a CAPACITY question. It is a LIFETIME question, and the number that settles it is not 64. **THE EXACT FIRST FAILING ALLOCATION, recovered from new `CNA_D3D12_DESCRIPTOR_TRACE` instrumentation rather than inferred from the check number:** the **65th** CBV/SRV/UAV request of the process, at which point the allocator's own counters read `cursor=30 live=30 alloc=64 free=34` after 78 submissions — the pool of 64 was full **while only 30 sampleable resources were alive**, because **34 of the 64 occupied slots belonged to resources that had already been destroyed**. `AllocateCbvSrvUavDescriptorEXT` and its RTV/DSV/SAMPLER siblings were monotonic bump cursors with **no free list and no growth**, and `D3D12TextureBackend`, `D3D12TextureCubeBackend`, `D3D12Texture3DBackend`, `D3D12RenderTargetBackend` and `D3D12RenderTargetCubeBackend` had **no destructor at all** — each takes one CBV/SRV/UAV slot at construction (a cube target additionally **six** RTVs and a DSV) and returned none. **THE PROOF THAT THIS HAD BEEN PAID FOR RATHER THAN FIXED** is the constant's own history in the header comment this task replaced: **8 -> 32 -> 48 -> 64**, every raise triggered by the test suite growing. **A SECOND HEAP FAILS IDENTICALLY:** REMED-GFX-170's ordinal fixture died pre-fix on an uncaught `SAMPLER descriptor heap exhausted` after 26 checks. **THE D3D12 RULE THAT DECIDED THE DESIGN:** `CopyDescriptors` may not read from a shader-visible source, so a shader-visible heap cannot be copied into its own larger successor — which is why the naive "grow and re-copy" is illegal and the staging heap exists. **FIX = two allocators in one new file, because the two heap classes have genuinely different constraints.** (a) RTV/DSV are never bound as a heap (`OMSetRenderTargets` takes loose CPU handles), so capacity grows by **appending a block**, each as large as everything before it — an issued handle **never moves**, which is exactly what lets the render-target classes keep storing a raw CPU handle; (b) CBV/SRV/UAV and SAMPLER must be contiguous and bound whole, so the authoritative copy of every descriptor lives in a **non-shader-visible staging heap** mirrored into the shader-visible one, growth re-mirrors the live range **from the staging heap** (a legal source), and callers hold a **stable INDEX**, never a handle — `GetShaderResourceViewGpuHandleEXT()` resolves against whichever heap is current on every call. Both reclaim through fence-stamped free lists; retired heap pairs are held the same way; `D3D12SamplerCache` stores an index for the same reason. **THE STARTING CAPACITIES ARE DELIBERATELY UNCHANGED — 64, 16, 64, 8** — the correction is reclamation and bounded doubling to the D3D12 specification's own ceiling, **not a larger constant**. **NOTHING ADDED PER DRAW:** no device idle, no queue wait, no extra submit, no extra public frame, no retry, no heap per draw or per frame; the only new per-descriptor cost is one `CopyDescriptorsSimple(1, ...)` at descriptor CREATION (`publishes == allocations`, asserted). **RESULTS, red-first by narrow restoration of the twelve production files: new `examples/descriptor_capacity_contract_test.cpp` 2/15 -> 29/29 on D3D12** (pre-fix twelve of thirteen legs killed outright), **new `examples/d3d12_descriptor_allocator_test.cpp` 55/55**, and **REMED-GFX-175's own fixture 64-checks-then-abort -> 87/87**, which finally completes the measurement that ticket had to leave open. GFX-170 25/26 -> 69/70, GFX-150 127+abort -> 144/146 (same 2 pre-existing FAILs, A/B-proven identical), smoke 253/253 -> 260/260. **THRESHOLDS 1/2/31/32/63/64/65/66/96/128/256 ALL GREEN**, plus 65 live render targets sampled back, 96 DualTexture, 70 AlphaTest, 70 EnvironmentMap, 96 SpriteBatch flushes and 96 sprites in one batch, indexed/non-indexed/DrawUser, 8x40 acquire-release rounds, 100-destroyed-then-100-recreated, 200 interleaved churn steps and 70 live TextureCubes. **THE ORACLE IS SELF-IDENTIFYING, not a colour comparison:** texture *i* is 2x2 RGBA whose twelve 0/255-only channels spell *i* in binary and decode back to an integer, so an aliased slot **names the wrong resource** instead of producing a near-miss shade — 0 wrong everywhere, 0 draws decoding as a destroyed generation. **CARDINALITY over the public fixture (17084 traced events): 5435 CBV/SRV/UAV allocations of which 5177 RECYCLED, peak live 258, capacity 512 after three doublings, 2 heap objects; RTV 340/274 recycled in 2 blocks; SAMPLER 47 distinct states, 0 recycles.** Pre-fix that same workload would have needed 5435 slots, **85x the capacity**. **STEADY STATE:** 500 churn steps around 50 live textures grow capacity by nothing and advance the cursor by nothing; 8 frames x 40 textures with a real per-frame fence signal each leave the live count where it started; a second wave of 300 takes **all 300 from the free list**. **VALIDATION, honestly bounded:** the Microsoft debug layer is **unavailable** under this vkd3d-proton runtime, so GPU-based validation and DRED are **not claimed**; loading the Khronos Vulkan layers into the Wine process was attempted and **did not work**, so that is reported as unavailable rather than clean; what was available, vkd3d-proton's own `VKD3D_DEBUG=info`, produced **zero errors, warnings or fixmes**. **SANITIZERS:** D3D12 is a MinGW/Wine cross-build with no ASan/UBSan runtime and the allocator needs a real `ID3D12Device`, so that boundary is **stated, not worked around**; | `REMED-GFX-181` | **Bgfx omitted `_flags` at the `EnvironmentMapEffect` cube binding, so `SamplerStates[1]` never reached the reflection cube — DONE (2026-07-31).** The ticket is RIGHT, names the mechanism exactly, and the correction is the two-line one it implies. What it could not say, and what the fix had to be built on, is **what bgfx does with an omitted `_flags`**: `bgfx::setTexture(_stage, _sampler, _handle, _flags)` defaults `_flags` to `UINT32_MAX`, and bgfx reads that as a **SENTINEL, not as a flag word** — `EncoderImpl::setTexture` (`bgfx_p.h:3440`) stores `BGFX_SAMPLER_INTERNAL_DEFAULT` (0x10000000) whenever that bit is present in the argument, and every renderer then resolves the binding as `0 == (BGFX_SAMPLER_INTERNAL_DEFAULT & _flags) ? _flags : m_flags` (`renderer_gl.cpp:6278` `getSamplerState`, `:6414` `TextureGL::commit`; `renderer_vk.cpp:4357`). No new state, no new capture, no second table. **THE FIX IS THE FIVE-ARGUMENT OVERLOAD WITH THAT SAME WORD, AT BOTH SITES — TWO LINES OF BEHAVIOUR IN THE WHOLE TASK.** **SUBMIT-SITE INVENTORY VERIFIED, NOT ASSUMED: the count really is two.** `DrawPrimitivesEx` and `DrawIndexedPrimitivesEx` are the only functions on this backend that bind a cube at all — `grep setTexture` returns exactly four call sites (two generic, via `BindSamplerSlot`/SpriteBatch, and these two), and `DrawInstancedPrimitivesEx` has **no env-map branch**, always submitting `instanced3DProgram_`. All eight public draw routes (DrawPrimitives, indexed 16/32-bit, nonzero startIndex+baseVertex, DrawUser non-indexed and indexed 16/32-bit, twice-written dynamic buffer) funnel through those same two. **COMMAND-STATE MODEL:** Bgfx submits immediately (`SubmitViewProgram` → `bgfx::submit`) and **bgfx itself** snapshots the encoder's bind state into the frame's command buffer at that call, so reading `samplerFlags_[1]` there **IS** the per-draw capture — the identical model the base slot has always used. Zero texture creations changed (the four creation sites now *store* the same expression they already passed, and nothing branches on it), zero recreations, zero extra draw/submit/view/pass/frame/wait/readback, zero per-draw allocation. **FALSE-POSITIVE AUDIT: 15 pre-existing Bgfx-registered fixtures drive `EnvironmentMapEffect` or the cube path and ALL 15 PASSED ON THE UNFIXED BACKEND. | `REMED-GFX-186` | **SDL_GPU SEGFAULTED reading a nonzero mip level of a mipmapped MULTISAMPLED `RenderTarget2D` — DONE (2026-07-31). THE TICKET SAID "MULTISAMPLED"; MULTISAMPLING WAS ONLY THE TRIGGER, AND THE SECOND HALF OF THE DEFECT NEEDED NEITHER MSAA NOR MIPS.** **PUBLIC CONTRACT ESTABLISHED FIRST, from FNA and FNA3D rather than from the crash:** `mipMap` and `multiSampleCount` are INDEPENDENT — FNA's `RenderTarget2D` forwards `mipMap` to its `Texture2D` base regardless of `preferredMultiSampleCount`, so `LevelCount` is the whole `CalculateMipLevels(w,h)` chain either way, and `FNA3D_ResolveTarget` (which runs at UNBIND) does both jobs in one required order: resolve the multisample buffer into the SINGLE-SAMPLE texture's level 0, then, `if (levelCount > 1)`, regenerate the chain from it. FNA3D's own SDL_GPU driver says it outright — `SDLGPU_ResolveTarget` returns early ONLY when `num_levels <= 1` ("Nothing to do, SDL_GPU resolves MSAA for us") and otherwise ends the pass and calls `SDL_GenerateMipmapsForGPUTexture` on the single-sample texture, while `SDLGPU_GenColorRenderbuffer` gives the multisample attachment ONE level. So: N public levels, level 0 resolved at unbind, levels 1..N-1 generated at the same moment, `GetData(level>0)` supported and synchronous, every level owned by the RESOLVED texture, and `level >= LevelCount` a caller error rejected before any native call. **EXACT PRE-FIX FAILURE:** SIGSEGV, exit 139, core dumped; ASan `SEGV on unknown address 0x000000000040 ... READ ... address points to the zero page`, thread T0 — the caller's own thread — pc inside `VULKAN_DownloadFromTexture`. **THE EXACT FAILING EXPRESSION**, read from the vendored SDL3 3.5.0 tree: `VULKAN_INTERNAL_FetchTextureSubresource` returns `&subresources[mip_level + layer*num_levels]` = `&subresources[1]` of a ONE-element array (`SDL_gpu_vulkan.c:4371`), and the first use of that slot reads `texture->usage` in `VULKAN_INTERNAL_DefaultTextureUsageMode` (`SDL_gpu_vulkan.c:2880`) — **offset 0x40 of a null `VulkanTexture*`**, matching the reported fault address exactly. CNA frame `SdlGpuGraphicsBackend.cpp:6415`, public frame `Texture2D::GetData(int, const Rectangle*, Color*, int, int)`. **SDL's debug mode could never have caught it:** `SDL_DownloadFromGPUTexture` validates only null pointers even with `debug_mode` on. **TWO INDEPENDENT DEFECTS, both required:** (1) the constructor suppressed the caller's `mipMap` whenever multisampling APPLIED (`mipMap_ = mipMap && multiSampleCount_ == 0`), so the resolved texture had ONE level while the public `LevelCount` still reported five — the comment claimed MSAA and mips are mutually exclusive "on the same attachment", but they are never on the same attachment: the chain belongs to the single-sample resolve texture and the multisample attachment twenty lines below is a separate `COLOR_TARGET`-only texture with `num_levels = 1`; (2) `GetData` never range-checked `level` at all, which crashed even a PLAIN non-mipmapped non-multisampled target read at level 1 (leg C3) — the sibling routes already had that guard (`SdlGpuRenderTargetCubeBackend` REMED-GFX-134, `SdlGpuTexture3DBackend`/`SdlGpuTextureCubeBackend` REMED-GFX-135), and the 2D target was the one route that never learned it, which is why an out-of-range level was a SIGNAL here and a public exception everywhere else. **THE TRACE IS WHAT MADE IT VISIBLE** (new `CNA_SDLGPU_TRACE_TARGET_READBACK`): pre-fix `appliedSamples=4 mipMap=0 nativeLevels=1 requestedLevel=1 levelDims=8x8 source=resolved mip_level=1 transferBytes=256` — every handle right, the resolved texture correctly selected, the level dimensions right and the transfer buffer correctly sized; the ONLY wrong number was `nativeLevels` against `LevelCount`. Post-fix the same line reads `mipMap=1 nativeLevels=5`. **FIX:** `mipMap_ = mipMap`, plus `levelCount_` recording what SDL really allocated and an `std::out_of_range` before any native call. Nothing had to be ADDED to make the chain real — `RenderToTarget` already resolves through the pass' `resolve_texture` store action and already calls `SDL_GenerateMipmapsForGPUTexture` after the pass ends, gated on exactly this flag. **No fabricated data, no MSAA/mipmap fallback, no priming read, no retry, no extra public frame, no Present, no dummy target switch, no sleep, no CPU mip generation, no public API change, no per-read resource recreation, and no extra submit or fence.** **A/B against the unmodified backend, same fixture: 8/49 legs -> 49/49, 41 SIGSEGVs -> 0**; the eight legs that survive pre-fix are exactly those that never reach the native copy with an out-of-range level. New process-isolated `examples/rendertarget_msaa_mip_readback_test.cpp`, **51 legs, 623 checks**, whose oracle is the level-0 four-quadrant image at each level's own size — falsifying "wrote nothing", "wrote zeroes" and "read level 0 instead" (a level-0 read of the same rectangle is entirely ONE quadrant) simultaneously. **MATRIX:** every level of 16x16, 13x7, 1x1, 2x2, 3x2, 5x3, 8x4; `level == LevelCount` and beyond, and a negative level; requested MSAA 0/1/2/4/8/16/64 with requested->applied printed (this device applies 0 for a requested 2, which is exactly why leg B2 passed pre-fix and B4 did not — the defect tracks the APPLIED count); mipmapped-without-MSAA, MSAA-without-mips and neither; all four DepthFormats; all three RenderTargetUsage values; ten readback shapes; repeated, reordered, two-target, disposal, teardown, six-cycle and post-exception reads. **VALIDATION:** SDL_GPU debug mode on and Vulkan validation PROVEN LOADED (`Loading layer library libVkLayer_khronos_validation.so`) — **0 VUID or validation messages across all 51 legs**. **SANITIZERS:** ASan+UBSan 51/51 legs, 623 checks, **0 AddressSanitizer errors, 0 UBSan runtime errors**; UBSan-only build likewise 51/51, 623, 0; runtimes proven linked by symbol (12 `__asan_report`, 14 `__ubsan_handle`). The LeakSanitizer residual is **624 B / 6 allocations with ZERO CNA frames**, byte-identical to an untouched SDL_GPU ASan fixture. **FALSE-POSITIVE AUDIT: five fixtures build a mipmapped `RenderTarget2D` and none would have failed; two strengthened with A/B evidence** — `sdlgpu_rendertarget2d_msaa_test.cpp`, the file NAMED for this backend's multisampled targets, had every target `mipMap=false` and every read at level 0 (**6/6 -> 9/9**; pre-fix it SIGSEGVs before printing a single check), and `rendertarget_msaa_first_readback_test.cpp`'s `kMsaaMipLevelProbeSafe` carve-out is DELETED rather than flipped (**34/34**, and pre-fix leg G1 reads level 0 exactly and then dies). Three more (`bound_target_lifetime`, `rendertarget_backbuffer_consumer` J2, `rendertarget_producer_consumer` F1) are recorded but NOT churned: all use sample count 0, so their chain was always allocated and they cannot fail. **CUBE AND MRT CLASSIFIED, NOT ABSORBED:** an MRT extra attachment is an ordinary `RenderTarget2D` on the SAME helper, so it is this ticket's route and leg I1 proves the fix covers it; a mipmapped multisampled `RenderTargetCube` has its OWN `GetData`, `levelCount_` and range check, never reached the out-of-bounds subresource, and REFUSES deterministically with the destination untouched — recorded separately. **CONTROLS, all 51/51 legs, exit 0: SDL_GPU 623 checks, EASYGL 629, VULKAN 612, BGFX 629, WEBGPU 67, SOFTWARE 382, HEADLESS 273** — WebGPU refuses mipmapped targets (WEBGPU-53/54), Software populates level 0 only and Headless refuses all target readback, each ASSERTED as a deterministic refusal that writes nothing rather than skipped. **SHARD:** `ctest -R '^SdlGpu' -j1` **105/105, exit 0, zero failures**, covering every named regression gate in the same run -- Texture2DMipStorage (GFX-176), TextureFilterMipContract (GFX-175), RenderTarget2DMSAA, MsaaFirstReadback (GFX-154), MsaaDepthContract (GFX-163), RenderTarget_PassBoundary (GFX-143/145), RenderTarget_DepthStencilUsage (GFX-142), Texture2D_GetDataTransferRange (GFX-149), RenderTargetCube_GetDataContract (GFX-134), EnvMapCubeSamplerContract (GFX-173), BackbufferReadbackDimension (GFX-165), RenderTarget_EffectSource (GFX-152), RenderTarget_ProducerConsumer, RenderTarget_BackbufferConsumer and RenderTarget_FirstUse. **Only SDL_GPU production changed** (one source file and its header). **FOUR NEW FINDINGS, each with its own ID and each a boundary that FAILS WHEN IT IS FIXED: `REMED-GFX-187`** (SDL3 3.5.0's `VULKAN_GenerateMipmaps` blits into `dim >> level` with no `max(1,...)` clamp, so a level whose destination extent is zero on either axis is never written; the D3D12 driver's `SDL_max(...,1)` one directory away proves it is the Vulkan driver specifically, and legs H7/H8/H9 reproduce it at applied sample count 0, proving it independent of MSAA and of this ticket); **`REMED-GFX-188`** (the mipmapped multisampled SDL_GPU cube reports `LevelCount=5` with only level 0 allocated); **`REMED-GFX-189`** (Vulkan rejects no out-of-range level at all, so `vkCmdCopyImageToBuffer` runs with an out-of-range `imageSubresource.mipLevel` and the call then WRITES the caller's destination and returns, the fabrication REMED-GFX-127/130 forbid — **since CLOSED 2026-07-31, which corrected two details measured here: the copy extent is (1,1,1), not (0,0,0), and FIVE VUIDs fire, led by `-imageSubresource-07967`, not one**); **`REMED-GFX-190`** (Vulkan does not regenerate the PRIMARY MRT attachment's chain, while the identical target after a single-target cycle is byte-exact). test `a31237d7`, fix `438b157f`, false positives + classification `1d9e0aba`, closure (this) | MEDIUM | GRAPHICS | DONE |
| `REMED-GFX-187` | **SDL_GPU/Vulkan now generates the complete public `RenderTarget2D` mip chain when one axis bottoms out first — DONE (2026-08-01).** **PUBLIC CONTRACT ESTABLISHED BEFORE THE FIX:** FNA's `Texture.CalculateMipLevels` starts at one level and halves the largest dimension until it reaches one; `Texture2D` sets `LevelCount = mipMap ? CalculateMipLevels(width,height) : 1`; and both whole-level `SetData` and `GetData` use `Math.Max(base >> level, 1)` independently on each axis. CNA mirrors all three facts: `RenderTarget2D` derives its public count independently of MSAA, `Texture2D::mipDim` is `max(1, base >> level)`, and valid indices are exactly `[0, LevelCount)`. Thus a mipmapped WxH target owns exactly N declared levels, level L is `max(1,W >> L)` by `max(1,H >> L)`, and the last declared level is 1x1. The vendored SDL snapshot `release-3.4.0-685-gcbe3fbe9f` exposed the integration mismatch: Vulkan's `VULKAN_GenerateMipmaps` iterates every native level but assigns raw `width >> level` and `height >> level` to `VkImageBlit` offsets, while the sibling D3D12 implementation uses SDL's public `SDL_BlitGPUTexture` with `SDL_max(...,1)` for both source and destination. **PRE-FIX A/B:** the same 5x3 target produced valid 5x3 and 2x1 levels but an all-zero final 1x1 level at applied MSAA 4 and 0; 8x4 level 3 and 13x7 level 3 had the same signature, proving the defect independent of resolve/MSAA. **NARROW FIX:** only CNA's SDL_GPU 2D render-target integration changed. After the existing render pass ends—and therefore after an MSAA attachment has resolved into the single-sample color texture—`GenerateRenderTargetMipChain` issues one GPU `SDL_BlitGPUTexture` for each destination level 1 through `levelCount-1`. Each blit reads the preceding declared level and sets source and destination extents with `max(1, base >> level)`; the loop bound is the native count already proven equal to the public `LevelCount`. Level 0 remains the rendered/resolved image, so the complete texture contains exactly N levels; no missing level is allocated or fabricated, no level is dropped, and `level == LevelCount` remains rejected. Primary and existing MRT-extra `RenderTarget2D` attachments use the helper; cube and ordinary-texture generation calls are unchanged, so REMED-GFX-188 was not begun. The helper remains on the existing command buffer, outside every pass, with the existing resolve-before-mips order: zero CPU mip generation, extra public frames, `Present`, retries, priming operations, submits, fences, public API changes, or other-backend production changes. The pre-existing `levelCount > 1` gate remains, and traced 1x1 MSAA/non-MSAA legs each emitted zero generation rows, preserving REMED-GFX-193. **DIRECT TRACE/CONTENT PROOF:** 5x3 emits exactly two rows, 5x3 -> 2x1 and 2x1 -> 1x1, on applied MSAA 4 and 0; every read window is written, every palette texel stays in its bounded nonzero envelope, derivable clean footprints retain their exact color, and the final level is no longer `(0,0,0,0)`. The strengthened process-isolated matrix reads every level and rejects each chain's own N for 1x13, 13x1, 2x13, 13x2, 3x2, 5x3, 8x4, and 13x7 on supported MSAA-resolved and non-MSAA routes. It additionally performs A/B/A regeneration across 13x7 and 8x4 targets, immediate disposal, survival to device teardown, and two render/sample/read cycles in which extreme 13x7-to-1x1 sampling is byte-equal to direct final-mip readback. The old SDL_GPU all-zero carve-outs are deleted rather than inverted. **SDL_GPU DEBUG + VULKAN VALIDATION:** backend debug mode was active; the Khronos validation library was proven inserted and loaded; focused traces, the full 63-leg/848-check mip fixture, and the 16-leg/689-check invalid-level fixture passed with zero VUID or validation diagnostics. **SANITIZERS:** ASan+UBSan ran the full suites with `halt_on_error`; 62/63 mip legs passed in the aggregate run, the sole H19 result was a pre-device `SDL_InitSubSystem` X11-availability miss and passed 11/11 immediately in isolation under the identical environment; invalid-level was 16/16; zero AddressSanitizer reports and zero UBSan runtime errors. Independent UBSan-only runs were 63/63 and 16/16 with zero runtime errors; sanitizer runtimes were confirmed linked. Leak detection alone remained disabled for the previously established driver-only residual. **REGRESSION/CONTROL PROOF:** focused `SdlGpu_TextureFilterMipContract` (GFX-175) passed 87/87, `SdlGpu_Texture2DMipStorage` (GFX-176) 112/112, and render-target sampling/orientation 62/62; the corrected serial SDL_GPU shard passed **106/106**, including those gates plus `SdlGpu_MsaaMipReadback` (GFX-186), MSAA allocation/first-read/depth, pass-boundary, producer/consumer, first-use, effect-source, lifetime, cube, texture readback, and backbuffer controls. The shard's first attempt was invalidated by its stale generated `DISPLAY=:157` entry (pre-device skips/aborts only); after changing only the reused build directory's test-display cache to the verified `:101`, the complete rerun passed with no skip. Full shared-fixture controls passed on EasyGL 63/63 + 16/16 and the independent Vulkan backend 63/63 + 16/16 with validation enabled; neither backend's production changed. Stable in-repository Debug, ASan+UBSan, and UBSan build trees were reused with ccache, one compilation lane at no more than `-j4`, serial GPU execution, and an observed peak of 83.8 C below the 84–85 C pause threshold. `audit/` is untouched. **NEW FINDINGS: none.** REMED-GFX-175, 176, 186, and 193 remain green; REMED-GFX-188 and every other ticket were not begun. Closure is in this signed commit. | LOW | GRAPHICS | DONE (2026-08-01; fix/test/closure in this signed commit) |
| `REMED-GFX-188` | **SDL_GPU mipmapped multisampled `RenderTargetCube` now owns and finalizes its complete public chain — DONE (2026-08-01).** **CONTRACT, established from local FNA/FNA3D before changing CNA:** `RenderTargetCube` passes `mipMap` to its `TextureCube` base independently of `preferredMultiSampleCount`; the resolved single-sample cube therefore owns the full `CalculateMipLevels(size,size)` chain, while `FNA3D_GenColorRenderbuffer` creates a separate one-level multisample resource and `FNA3D_ResolveTarget` resolves level 0 before generating lower levels. **PRE-FIX:** the initial focused oracle measured public/native `5/1` at applied 4x MSAA, safely refused every nonzero-level read, and produced stale level-0 values when minified in the same frame: **1227/1483** checks plus the teardown assertion failed, **257 `[FAIL]` lines**, no crash. **FIX, SDL_GPU ONLY:** the constructor no longer suppresses `mipMap` when MSAA applies; `SdlGpuRenderTargetCubeState::levelCount` records the resolved cube's real `num_levels` and is the single allocation/finalization/readback fact; the existing six per-face MSAA 2D attachments remain `num_levels=1`; each pass still resolves its own attachment into level 0 of `resolve_layer=face`, then, immediately after that pass ends, `GenerateCubeRenderTargetMipChain` issues clamped GPU `SDL_BlitGPUTexture` operations from level L-1 to L on that same cube layer. The former once-per-cube whole-resource generator is removed, so a later face or consumer pass cannot run before the just-rendered face is finalized. | `REMED-GFX-189` | **Vulkan did not reject a `RenderTarget2D::GetData` mip level outside the declared chain, and FABRICATED a result for it — DONE (2026-07-31). THE TICKET RECORDED FABRICATION; THE SAME MISSING GUARD ALSO PRODUCED AN UNCATCHABLE SIGSEGV, AND THE MAGNITUDE OF THE INVALID LEVEL IS WHAT SELECTS WHICH.** **ROOT CAUSE, one line:** `VulkanRenderTargetBackend::GetData` range-checked `level` not at all. The sibling routes on this very backend already did — `VulkanTexture3DBackend::GetData`, `VulkanTextureCubeBackend::GetData` and `VulkanRenderTargetCubeBackend::GetData` all test `level < 0` or `level >= levelCount_` — and the 2D target was the ONE route that never learned it, exactly the shape REMED-GFX-186 closed on SDL_GPU. Every fabricated answer measured is **(250,25,15,255) — LEVEL ZERO's texel (0,0)**, so "clamped to the final valid level" is FALSIFIED rather than merely unlikely. Leg B1 confirms it independently on a NON-mipmapped target where level 0 is the only level that exists at all. It is **not** stale staging memory either: the new `CNA_VULKAN_TARGET_READBACK_TRACE` samples the host-visible allocation immediately before the copy — `preCopyStaging=(0,64,25,87)` → returned `(250,25,15,255)`, i.e. BGRA `(15,25,250,255)` — so the copy genuinely executes and genuinely writes. **TWO CORRECTIONS TO THIS TICKET'S OWN EARLIER EVIDENCE, both measured:** the copy extent is **(1,1,1), NOT (0,0,0)** — "(0,0,0)" is the SUBRESOURCE size at the nonexistent level, quoted inside the validation text, not the extent CNA passes — and **FIVE** VUIDs fire per invalid request, not one: `07967` (`mipLevel is 5, but image has 5 mip levels`, the real one), plus `07970`, `07971`, `07972` and `09104`. **FIX:** `if (level < 0)` or `level >= levelCount_` then `throw std::out_of_range(...)`, placed AHEAD of the capability test per REMED-GFX-162's precedence (a specific public argument error is answered with its own error before a storage/capability decision can weaken it into a generic refusal). It THROWS rather than reporting `false`, because reporting false would raise `System::NotSupportedException` and tell a caller their backend cannot read render targets — untrue, and it hides the real mistake. Message: `CNA Vulkan: RenderTarget2D::GetData: mip level 1 does not exist (this target has 1 level)` (singular/plural correct). `std::out_of_range` matches the shared `Texture2D::GetData`, which already raises it for the NEGATIVE end of exactly this range, and the SDL_GPU sibling. **A/B, same fixture, same device: 1/16 legs → 16/16, exit 1 → 0, 2 SIGSEGVs → 0.** **NATIVE ZERO-WORK, measured not asserted:** 59 invalid requests → 59 `rt2d.read.reject` lines, and for those requests zero subresource lookups, zero layout transitions, zero staging allocations, zero command buffers, zero copies, zero submits, zero waits, zero destination writes. The five invalid-subresource VUIDs go **59 each → 0 each**. Post-fix `rt2d.read.native` and `rt2d.read.exit` are equal (69), so every native copy that starts also completes. Valid-request cardinality is unchanged: one staging buffer, one command buffer, one copy, one submit, one wait per read. No clamping, no fallback, no fabricated zeroes, no retry, no extra frame, no Present, no sleep, no public API change. **NEW FIXTURE:** process-isolated `examples/rendertarget_invalid_mip_level_test.cpp`, **16 legs**, registered on all seven backends, whose refusal oracle is a sentinel-filled destination with protected prefix and suffix — clamping, level 0, zero-fill and staging memory all fail it identically, so the check does not need to know WHICH wrong answer a backend would invent. **MATRIX:** invalid levels `N`, `N+1`, `INT_MAX`, `-1`, on mipmapped 13×7 and non-mipmapped 16×16; invalid level with full rectangle, null rectangle, sub-rectangle, `startIndex` 0 and 17, and an undersized destination; ten repeated invalid requests; valid chains 1×1, 2×2, 3×2, 5×3, 8×4, 13×7, 16×16 with every level read; nine readback shapes; MSAA 0/1/2/4/8; DepthFormat None/16/24/24S8; all three `RenderTargetUsage`; first-request-invalid, invalid↔valid interleaving, two targets, A/B/A, four create/read/refuse/dispose cycles with object-address reuse, disposal immediately after a refusal, and survival to device teardown. **CROSS-BACKEND CONTROLS, all 16/16 legs, exit 0: VULKAN 519 checks, SDL_GPU 511, EASYGL 461, BGFX 461, SOFTWARE 324, HEADLESS 260, WEBGPU 96.** **SANITIZERS:** ASan 519/519 checks, **zero AddressSanitizer memory errors**; the 368 B / 4-allocation LeakSanitizer residual has **zero CNA frames** and is **byte-identical to an untouched Vulkan ASan fixture** (`cna_test_vulkan_env_map`, 368 B / 4), so it is pre-existing driver behaviour.
